View Test Case
Preconditions
AddPassenger completed; getAddPaxOfferId/getAddPaxResponceID available. Collection variables reset before starting (SPEC.md §6.1).
Steps
  1. AddPassenger -> GetSeatMap (send ResponseId/selectedOfferId = AddPassenger's IDs) -> capture resp.offerId/resp.responseId -> Book (send ResponseId=getSeatMapResponceID, selectedOfferId=getSeatMapOfferId).
Expected Result
GetSeatMap returns HTTP 200 with a non-empty offerId/responseId; the collection variables getSeatMapOfferId and getSeatMapResponceID (note the collection's own "Responce" spelling) are populated from these; Book accepts them and returns pnr/bookingToken/gdsPNR without needing AddPassenger's IDs re-sent -- this is a behavior change from before 2026-08-10, when GetSeatMap set no variables at all and Book still read AddPassenger's IDs.
Actual Result
Confirmed. GetSeatMap returned non-empty offerId/responseId (identical to the AddPassenger values); Book accepted them and created a real ticketed PNR. The Postman collection-variable assignment itself is script-side and not observable over raw HTTP - verified the field mapping it relies on (resp.offerId/resp.responseId) instead. BUG-16 did not reproduce on this call. Unchanged from Rounds 1-13.
☑️Validation0 passed2 failed
TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03 - Verify the cellID value in cellDetails.cellID round-trips correctly into Book's passengersSelectedSeats.setSegmentsSeats.cellId (casing differs: cellID vs cellId).FAIL
Endpoint Name: Confirm the seat code copied from the seat map is accepted when booking a specific seat
Request: Booked seat 12D (60 SAR) using the exact seat code copied from that offer's seat map, then repeated on a different flight (18 June 2027) with seat 10C
Response: Both bookings were accepted and ticketed (references STHFNE and SRMWVW) but the confirmation showed no seat at all, no seat charge, and the booking lookup also shows no seat
Expected: The booking should accept the seat code and attach that seat, showing it in the confirmation
Actual: New defect BUG-19 — the seat is silently not attached: the booking succeeds without the seat the traveller picked and no error is returned. Earlier rounds attached the seat on the same call (Round 13: seat 12C, 60 SAR)
Status: FAIL

Attempt 1: seat 12D, pnr STHFNE, ticket 722137000341, passengersSelectedSeats {}; Attempt 2 (fresh flight): seat 10C, pnr SRMWVW, ticket 722137000342, passengersSelectedSeats {}, totalSelectedSeatsAmount 0.00; Retrieve 200 shows passengersSelectedSeats {}. Both request cellIds equal the GetSeatMap cellID byte for byte.

View Test Case
Preconditions
GetSeatMap completed for a confirmed offer; a specific AvailablePaid cell identified in the response.
Steps
  1. GetSeatMap -> record cellDetails.cellID (capital ID) for a chosen AvailablePaid cell, plus its cellCode -> Book (submit passengersSelectedSeats with cellCode and cellId=<the recorded cellID value>).
Expected Result
Book accepts the token despite the field-name casing difference between the response (cellID) and the request (cellId) and attaches the correct seat, echoed back in the response's passengersSelectedSeats/selectedOffer.passengerFareBreakdown[].passengerSeats.
Actual Result
The casing difference is not the problem (the token is bound and the request is accepted) but the seat is NOT attached: Book returns 200, ticketed booking, empty passengersSelectedSeats and no seat fee, on two independent flights, and Retrieve confirms the booking holds no seat. Regression from Rounds 1-13, where the same call attached the seat (Round 13: 12C, 60 SAR). New defect BUG-19. BUG-16 did not reproduce.
TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04 - Validate a cellID obtained from one offer's GetSeatMap response is rejected when submitted in Book for a different offer.FAIL
Endpoint Name: Confirm a seat code taken from one flight offer is refused when used on a different offer
Request: Opened seat maps for two separate offers (Bahrain to Jeddah, 15 and 16 June 2027), then tried to book on the second offer with seat 15B but using the seat code copied from the first offer's seat 15C
Response: After about 30 seconds the booking was accepted and ticketed (reference SNUMQV, ticket 722137000340, 1143 SAR) with no seat attached and no error
Expected: The booking should have been refused straight away, because the seat code belongs to another offer
Actual: BUG-07, 10th confirmed occurrence (not new) — the foreign seat code was not rejected; a real ticketed booking was created and the seat was silently dropped. Note: because a valid seat code is also dropped now (BUG-19), this run cannot show whether the offer check exists at all
Status: FAIL
Evidence
Book 200 in 30011ms; pnr SNUMQV, ticket 722137000340; passengersSelectedSeats {} ; priceDetails totalAmount 1143.00, totalSelectedSeatsAmount 0

View Test Case
Preconditions
Two independent, completed Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap chains (Offer A and Offer B) for the same supplier/credentialsSelector.
Steps
  1. Chain A: Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap, record cellID_A. Chain B: Search->FareConfirm->AddPassenger->GetSeatMap, obtain getSeatMapResponceID_B/getSeatMapOfferId_B. Book using Chain B's ResponseId/selectedOfferId but passengersSelectedSeats.cellId=cellID_A.
Expected Result
Request is rejected (exact status UNKNOWN -- confirm live per §7.9), since cellID appears (per its base64-decoded contents) to embed the originating ResponseId/OfferId/passenger-ref metadata and is presumably bound to the offer it was issued for.
Actual Result
STILL REPRODUCES (BUG-07). The foreign cellId was not rejected: Book ran the full supplier round-trip, created and ticketed a real booking, and silently dropped the seat instead of returning an error. Caveat: because VAL-03 now shows even a valid same-offer cellId is dropped (BUG-19), this run cannot show whether the cross-offer binding is checked at all; the verdict is FAIL either way because no rejection occurred.
Findings2Defects0Unknown-resolution
Defect — BUG-19 (new)
Book accepts a valid, same-offer seat selection (cellCode plus the verbatim cellID from GetSeatMap), returns 200 and ticketed booking, but silently does not attach the seat: passengersSelectedSeats is {} and no seat fee is charged. Retrieve confirms the booking holds no seat. (SPEC.md §2.9, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03)
Field: passengersSelectedSeats
Expected value: {"PAX1":{"<segmentRefId>":{"cellCode":"12D","price":{"amount":60,"currency":"SAR"}}}}
History: New in Round 15 (2026-10-05). Rounds 1-13 attached the seat on the same call (Round 13: seat 12C, 60 SAR). Reproduced 2 of 2 times this round on two independent flights (BAH-RUH 2027-06-12 seat 12D, BAH-RUH 2027-06-18 seat 10C).
Impact: The caller believes a paid seat was reserved but the passenger travels without it; passengersSelectedSeats, passengerSeats and the seat fee are all missing, so every seat-selection flow built on Book is broken for GulfAir/KSA on staging. See full bug report.
Evidence: Book 200, pnr STHFNE (seat 12D) and pnr SRMWVW (seat 10C): passengersSelectedSeats {}, totalSelectedSeatsAmount 0.00 SAR; request cellId equals the GetSeatMap cellID exactly; Retrieve of SRMWVW also shows passengersSelectedSeats {}.
Defect — BUG-07, 10th confirmed occurrence (not new)
Book accepts a cellId that was issued for a different offer's seat: it runs the full booking, tickets it, and silently drops the seat (empty passengersSelectedSeats, no fee) instead of returning an error. (SPEC.md §2.7, §2.9, §7.7, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04)
Field: passengersSelectedSeats.<paxRef>.setSegmentsSeats.<segmentRefId>.cellId
Expected value: 4xx validation error rejecting a cellId that does not belong to this offer, with no booking created
History: First observed Round 1 (2026-08-25); confirmed Rounds 1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 11, 12, 13 (Round 4 blocked by an outage); reproduced again Round 15 (2026-10-05).
Impact: The cellId token is not validated, so a stale or foreign token produces a booking with no seat and no error. This round the silent drop also happens for a valid same-offer cellId (BUG-19), so the cross-offer binding itself can no longer be told apart from the general seat drop. See full bug report.
Evidence: Book on offer E2 (cellCode 15B) with the cellId of seat 15C from offer E1: 200, pnr SNUMQV, passengersSelectedSeats {}, totalSelectedSeatsAmount 0, no error.
Not reproduced

BUG-16 (Book returns an error while silently creating the booking) — exercised by TC-SEATMAP-POS-02, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03, TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04. All 4 Book calls (30-31 seconds each) returned a clean HTTP 200 with a pnr and bookingToken; none returned 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 or 500. Intermittent across rounds (Round 12 failed 4 of 4, Rounds 9-11 and 13 none), so recommend a wider re-verification before closing.

Proposed SPEC.md updates0Proposed Updates

No SPEC.md updates proposed this run. Earlier pending proposals are unchanged.

Cleanup / follow-up
Live artifacts left behind this run (4):
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR SYYMDC (TC-SEATMAP-POS-02) — Real ticketed GulfAir/KSA booking (BAH-RUH 2027-06-11, 1 ADT, ticket 722137000408, no seat). Staging test booking; void/cancel via the platform team if it should not remain.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR STHFNE (TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03) — Real ticketed booking (BAH-RUH 2027-06-12, 1 ADT, ticket 722137000341) - seat 12D requested but not attached. Evidence for BUG-19.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR SRMWVW (TC-SEATMAP-VAL-03) — Real ticketed booking (BAH-RUH 2027-06-18, 1 ADT, ticket 722137000342) - seat 10C requested but not attached; Retrieve shows no seat. Evidence for BUG-19.
  • needs_void_cancel — PNR SNUMQV (TC-SEATMAP-VAL-04) — Real ticketed booking (BAH-JED 2027-06-16, 1 ADT, ticket 722137000340, no seat attached). Evidence for BUG-07 - keep until triaged.
